- **forgemaster-auto**: Same four phases as `/forgemaster` — **research → plan → implement → ship** — but every phase reviews its own output with a **fresh evaluator subagent**, feeds the findings back, revises, and **advances itself**. - **forgemaster-mini**: Run the **low-ceremony** Forge path for small, clear-scope work (bugs, chores, config, single-file changes). - **format-patch**: `libra format-patch` 将提交导出为 mbox 格式的邮件补丁文件。该命令解析 `A..B` 修订范围（空左右侧均默认 `HEAD`，单修订号按 `&lt;spec&gt;..HEAD` 处理），对范围内的每个非合并提交生成一个补丁文件（文件名后缀由 `--suffix` 决定，默认 `.patch`；`--numbered-files` 下改用纯序号并忽略后缀）。除 `--numbered-files` 外，文件名始终带补零序号前缀，不受 `-n`/`-N` 影响。每个文件按 RFC 2822 标准生成 mbox 信封（`From ` 行）、邮件头（`From:`、`Date:`、`Subject:`、`Message-ID` 等）和邮件体（提交正文 + `---` 分隔 + diffstat + unified diff）。输出通过 `-o` 写入目录或通过 `--stdout` ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/format-patch.md) · ★101)

- **fp-docs:help**: Display all fp-docs commands grouped by type with descriptions. Quick reference for discovering available documentation operations. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/help.md))
- **fp-docs:locals**: Manage locals contract documentation for WordPress template components. Subcommands: annotate, contracts, cross-ref, validate, shapes, coverage. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/locals.md))
- **fp-docs:parallel**: Run documentation operations in parallel across multiple files using Agent Teams. Opt-in feature — falls back to sequential if teams are disabled. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/parallel.md))
- **fp-docs:remediate**: Resolve audit findings by dispatching to the right specialist engines. Takes audit output or a saved plan and orchestrates batch remediation. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/remediate.md))
- **fp-docs:revise**: Fix specific documentation you know is wrong or outdated. Provide a description of what needs fixing and the engine will locate, update, and validate the affected docs. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/revise.md))
- **fp-docs:sanity-check**: Validate that documentation claims match actual source code. Zero-tolerance mode flags any discrepancy. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/sanity-check.md))
- **fp-docs:setup**: Initialize or verify the fp-docs plugin installation. Checks plugin structure, docs repo setup, codebase gitignore, and branch sync state. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/setup.md))
- **fp-docs:sync**: Synchronize the docs repo branch with the codebase branch. Creates or switches docs branches, generates diff reports, and optionally merges docs branches. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/sync.md))
- **fp-docs:test**: Execute runtime validations against the local development environment. Tests REST endpoints, WP-CLI commands, template rendering, and visual verification via browser automation. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/test.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-audit**: Audit user guide coverage gaps. Compare user-visible features in codebase against documented pages. Report undocumented features. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-audit.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-batch**: Batch operations across multiple user guide pages. Uses Agent Teams for parallel execution when enabled, falls back to sequential. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-batch.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-generate**: Generate a new user guide page for a feature or workflow. Analyzes code, discovers UI paths, captures screenshots, and writes content using the appropriate page template. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-generate.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-preview**: Start local Hugo dev server or trigger preview deploy for user guide on current branch. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-preview.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-screenshot**: Capture or refresh screenshots for user guide pages. Uses Playwright MCP to navigate WP admin and capture current UI state. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-screenshot.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-status**: Report user guide health metrics. Shows page counts, screenshot inventory, coverage percentage, staleness, and structural health. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-status.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-update**: Update an existing user guide page after codebase changes. Detects what changed, re-verifies UI paths, and refreshes stale screenshots. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-update.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-validate**: Validate user guide accuracy against current UI state. Checks navigation paths, screenshot currency, tone compliance, and content completeness. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-validate.md))
- **fp-docs:update**: Check for and install fp-docs plugin updates. Shows changelog from GitHub release notes, confirms before updating. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update.md))
- **fp-docs:update-claude**: Regenerate the CLAUDE.md template with current skill inventory, documentation links, and project configuration. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update-claude.md))
- **fp-docs:update-index**: Refresh the PROJECT-INDEX.md codebase reference. Scans source files and updates the master index. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update-index.md))
- **fp-docs:update-skills**: Regenerate all plugin skills from the current prompt definitions. Syncs skill files with the source-of-truth prompts. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update-skills.md))
- **fp-docs:verbosity-audit**: Scan existing documentation for verbosity gaps — missing items, summarization language, unexpanded enumerables. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/verbosity-audit.md))

- **fractal-think-harvest**: 1. **Evidence over narrative** - Every finding must trace to specific graph nodes; no unsupported claims. 2. **Contradictions are findings** - Unresolved tensions are reported as open questions, not hidden. 3. **Preserve synthesis fidelity** - Read bottom-up syntheses as composed by workers. Do not reconstruct, reinterpret, or replace them. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/fractal-think-harvest.md) · ★13)
- **fractal-think-harvest**: Phase 3 of fractal-thinking: Read completed graph, verify synthesis, format FractalResult ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/fractal-think-harvest.md) · ★13)
- **fractal-think-seed**: Seed phase of fractal-thinking: Create graph and generate seed sub-questions ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/fractal-think-seed.md) · ★13)
- **fractal-think-seed**: 1. **Seed determines root** - Every graph starts from exactly one seed node. 2. **No upfront clustering** - Questions are added as flat children of root. Branch structure emerges from recursive decomposition, not surface-level domain grouping. 3. **Budget set once** - Intensity determines max_agents and max_depth at creation; never change mid-exploration. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/fractal-think-seed.md) · ★13)
- **fractal-think-work**: Phase 2 of fractal-thinking: Dispatch workers that execute the recursive fractal_explore primitive on graph nodes ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/fractal-think-work.md) · ★13)
- **fractal-think-work**: 1. **The graph IS the work queue** - All state is in MCP tools. No separate task queue, no round counter, no cluster registry. Workers pull work dynamically via `fractal_claim_work`. 2. **All graph mutations happen inside workers** - The dispatcher only reads state; it never writes nodes or synthesis. 3. **Return, don't re-dispatch** - When workers exit with open work, return state to the orchestr ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/fractal-think-work.md) · ★13)
